Stock-market rules from the documents, tested on 26 years of the S&P 500 and the BSE Sensex (20 Nov 2000 – 20 Nov 2026)

Every general-market rule in TA, KD and the 2026 document that can be computed, scored like the gold rules: entry at the last close before the event in the exchange's own time, windows to their last close, instants on 5 sessions, FLAT under 0.5%, each rule compared with how often the index went that way over the same horizon in the same Nov–Nov year (the base). With several hundred rules tested, about 5% will show p < 0.05 by luck alone, so the false-discovery-rate q and the walk-forward column are the honest guides.
